│ #!/usr/bin/env python3 import torch import torch.nn as nn from               │
│ torch.utils.data import TensorDataset, DataLoader import os                  │
│                                                                              │
│ WEIGHTS_PATH = '/app/weights.pt' DATASET_PATH = '/app/dataset.pt'            │
│ OUTPUT_SCRIPT_PATH = '/app/model.pt'                                         │
│                                                                              │
│ def infer_layers_from_state(state: dict): prefixes = [] for key in           │
│ state.keys(): pref = key.split('.')[0] if pref not in prefixes:              │
│ prefixes.append(pref) layers = [] for pref in prefixes: w_key =              │
│ f'{pref}.weight' if w_key not in state: continue w = state[w_key] bias_key = │
│ f'{pref}.bias' has_bias = bias_key in state if w.dim() == 2: out_features,   │
│ in_features = int(w.shape[0]), int(w.shape[1]) linear =                      │
│ nn.Linear(in_features, out_features, bias=has_bias)                          │
│ linear.weight.data.copy_(state[w_key]) if has_bias:                          │
│ linear.bias.data.copy_(state[bias_key]) layers.append((pref, linear)) elif   │
│ w.dim() == 4: out_c, in_c, kH, kW = int(w.shape[0]), int(w.shape[1]),        │
│ int(w.shape[2]), int(w.shape[3]) conv = nn.Conv2d(in_c, out_c,               │
│ kernel_size=(kH, kW), bias=has_bias) conv.weight.data.copy_(state[w_key]) if │
│ has_bias: conv.bias.data.copy_(state[bias_key]) layers.append((pref, conv))  │
│ else: print(f"Warning: Unsupported weight dimension {w.dim()} for {pref},    │
│ skipping.") return layers                                                    │
│                                                                              │
│ class RecoveredModel(nn.Module): def init(self, layers_info): super().init() │
│ self.layer_names = [name for name, _ in layers_info] # Attach modules with   │
│ their original prefixes for name, mod in layers_info: setattr(self, name,    │
│ mod) self.output_layer = getattr(self, self.layer_names[-1])                 │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  def forward(self, x):                                                       │
│      for i, name in enumerate(self.layer_names):                             │
│          layer = getattr(self, name)                                         │
│          x = layer(x)                                                        │
│          if i != len(self.layer_names) - 1:                                  │
│              x = torch.relu(x)                                               │
│      return x                                                                │

│ torch.nn as nn\nfrom torch.utils.data import TensorDataset,                  │

│ \&#x27;/app/weights.pt\&#x27;\nDATASET_PATH =                                │
│ \&#x27;/app/dataset.pt\&#x27;\nOUTPUT_SCRIPT_PATH =                          │
│ \&#x27;/app/model.pt\&#x27;\n\ndef infer_layers_from_state(state: dict):\n   │
│ &quot;&quot;&quot;\n    Infer an ordered list of (name, module) from a flat  │
│ state_dict.\n    Keys are expected as &quot;&lt;prefix&gt;.weight&quot; and  │
│ &quot;&lt;prefix&gt;.bias&quot;.\n    Supports Linear (2D) and Conv2d        │
│ (4D).\n    Preserves the first-seen order of prefixes.\n    Returns: list of │
│ (prefix, module)\n    &quot;&quot;&quot;\n    prefixes = []\n    for key in  │
│ state.keys():\n        pref = key.split(\&#x27;.\&#x27;)[0]\n        if pref │
│ not in prefixes:\n            prefixes.append(pref)\n\n    layers = []\n     │
│ for pref in prefixes:\n        w_key = f\&#x27;{pref}.weight\&#x27;\n        │
│ if w_key not in state:\n            continue\n        w = state[w_key]\n     │
│ bias_key = f\&#x27;{pref}.bias\&#x27;\n        has_bias = bias_key in        │
│ state\n\n        if w.dim() == 2:\n            # Linear: [out_features,      │
│ in_features]\n            out_features, in_features = int(w.shape[0]),       │
│ int(w.shape[1])\n            linear = nn.Linear(in_features, out_features,   │
│ bias=has_bias)\n            linear.weight.data.copy_(state[w_key])\n         │
│ if has_bias:\n                linear.bias.data.copy_(state[bias_key])\n      │
│ layers.append((pref, linear))\n        elif w.dim() == 4:\n            #     │
│ Conv2d: [out_channels, in_channels, kH, kW]\n            out_c, in_c, kH, kW │
│ = int(w.shape[0]), int(w.shape[1]), int(w.shape[2]), int(w.shape[3])\n       │
│ conv = nn.Conv2d(in_c, out_c, kernel_size=(kH, kW), bias=has_bias)\n         │
│ conv.weight.data.copy_(state[w_key])\n            if has_bias:\n             │
│ conv.bias.data.copy_(state[bias_key])\n            layers.append((pref,      │
│ conv))\n        else:\n            print(f&quot;Warning: Unsupported weight  │
│ dimension {w.dim()} for {pref}, skipping.&quot;)\n    return layers\n\nclass │
│ RecoveredModel(nn.Module):\n    &quot;&quot;&quot;\n    Dynamically          │
│ constructed model where each layer is attached as a top-level attribute\n    │
│ with its original prefix name (e.g., layer1, layer2, output_layer).\n        │
│ Forward passes through the layers in the same order, applying ReLU between\n │
│ layers (except after the last one) if feasible.\n    &quot;&quot;&quot;\n    │
│ def __init__(self, layers_info):\n        super().__init__()\n               │
│ self.layer_names = []\n        for name, mod in layers_info:\n               │
│ setattr(self, name, mod)\n            self.layer_names.append(name)\n        │

╭────────────────────────────── solver - message ──────────────────────────────╮
│ I’ll provide a concise, robust, ready-to-run script you can execute in a     │
│ PyTorch-enabled environment. It reconstructs the original model from         │
│ /app/weights.pt, loads /app/dataset.pt, computes the initial MSE, freezes    │
│ all layers except the final output_layer and trains only that layer, then    │
│ saves a TorchScript model to /app/model.pt. It also includes a lightweight   │
│ post-check to confirm that only the output_layer differs between the         │
│ original weights and the saved model.                                        │
│                                                                              │
│ What you should run in a PyTorch-enabled environment                         │
│                                                                              │
│  • Install PyTorch if needed: pip install torch torchvision torchaudio       │
│  • Save the following as recover_and_tune.py                                 │
│  • Run: python recover_and_tune.py                                           │
│                                                                              │
│ Code: recover_and_tune.py                                                    │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  #!/usr/bin/env python3                                                      │
│  import torch                                                                │
│  import torch.nn as nn                                                       │
│  from torch.utils.data import TensorDataset, DataLoader                      │
│  import os                                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│  WEIGHTS_PATH = &#x27;/app/weights.pt&#x27;                                  │
│  DATASET_PATH = &#x27;/app/dataset.pt&#x27;                                  │
│  OUTPUT_SCRIPT_PATH = &#x27;/app/model.pt&#x27;                              │
│                                                                              │
│  def infer_layers_from_state(state: dict):                                   │
│      &quot;&quot;&quot;                                                      │
│      Infer an ordered list of (name, module) from a flat state_dict.         │
│      Keys are expected as &quot;&lt;prefix&gt;.weight&quot; and              │
│  &quot;&lt;prefix&gt;.bias&quot;.                                            │
│      Supports Linear (2D) and Conv2d (4D).                                   │
│      Preserves the first-seen order of prefixes.                             │
│      Returns: list of (prefix, module)                                       │
│      &quot;&quot;&quot;                                                      │
│      prefixes = []                                                           │
│      for key in state.keys():                                                │
│          pref = key.split(&#x27;.&#x27;)[0]                                  │
│          if pref not in prefixes:                                            │
│              prefixes.append(pref)                                           │
│                                                                              │
│      layers = []                                                             │
│      for pref in prefixes:                                                   │
│          w_key = f&#x27;{pref}.weight&#x27;                                  │
│          if w_key not in state:                                              │
│              continue                                                        │
│          w = state[w_key]                                                    │
│          bias_key = f&#x27;{pref}.bias&#x27;                                 │
│          has_bias = bias_key in state                                        │
│                                                                              │
│          if w.dim() == 2:                                                    │
│              # Linear: [out_features, in_features]                           │
│              out_features, in_features = int(w.shape[0]), int(w.shape[1])    │
│              linear = nn.Linear(in_features, out_features, bias=has_bias)    │
│              linear.weight.data.copy_(state[w_key])                          │
│              if has_bias:                                                    │
│                  linear.bias.data.copy_(state[bias_key])                     │
│              layers.append((pref, linear))                                   │
│          elif w.dim() == 4:                                                  │
│              # Conv2d: [out_channels, in_channels, kH, kW]                   │
│              out_c, in_c, kH, kW = int(w.shape[0]), int(w.shape[1]),         │
│  int(w.shape[2]), int(w.shape[3])                                            │
│              conv = nn.Conv2d(in_c, out_c, kernel_size=(kH, kW),             │
│  bias=has_bias)                                                              │
│              conv.weight.data.copy_(state[w_key])                            │
│              if has_bias:                                                    │
│                  conv.bias.data.copy_(state[bias_key])                       │
│              layers.append((pref, conv))                                     │
│          else:                                                               │
│              print(f&quot;Warning: Unsupported weight dimension {w.dim()}    │
│  for {pref}, skipping.&quot;)                                                │
│      return layers                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│  class RecoveredModel(nn.Module):                                            │
│      &quot;&quot;&quot;                                                      │
│      Dynamically constructed model where each layer is attached as a         │
│  top-level attribute                                                         │
│      with its original prefix name (e.g., layer1, layer2, output_layer).     │
│      Forward passes through the layers in the same order, applying ReLU      │
│  between                                                                     │
│      layers (except after the last one) if feasible.                         │
│      &quot;&quot;&quot;                                                      │
│      def __init__(self, layers_info):                                        │
│          super().__init__()                                                  │
│          self.layer_names = []                                               │
│          for name, mod in layers_info:                                       │
│              setattr(self, name, mod)                                        │
│              self.layer_names.append(name)                                   │
│          self.output_layer = getattr(self, self.layer_names[-1]) if          │
│  self.layer_names else None                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│      def forward(self, x):                                                   │
│          for i, name in enumerate(self.layer_names):                         │
│              layer = getattr(self, name)                                     │
│              x = layer(x)                                                    │
│              if i != len(self.layer_names) - 1:                              │
│                  x = torch.relu(x)                                           │
│          return x                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│  def main():                                                                 │
│      if not os.path.exists(WEIGHTS_PATH):                                    │
│          raise SystemExit(f&#x27;Weights file not found:                     │
│  {WEIGHTS_PATH}&#x27;)                                                       │
│      if not os.path.exists(DATASET_PATH):                                    │
│          raise SystemExit(f&#x27;Dataset file not found:                     │
│  {DATASET_PATH}&#x27;)                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│      # Load weights (state_dict)                                             │
│      state = torch.load(WEIGHTS_PATH, map_location=&#x27;cpu&#x27;)          │
│      if not isinstance(state, dict):                                         │
│          raise SystemExit(&#x27;Weights file does not contain a dict         │
│  (state_dict).&#x27;)                                                        │
│                                                                              │
│      # Reconstruct architecture from state dict                              │
│      layers_info = infer_layers_from_state(state)                            │
│      if not layers_info:                                                     │
│          raise SystemExit(&#x27;Failed to infer any layers from the          │
│  weights.&#x27;)                                                             │
│                                                                              │
│      model = RecoveredModel(layers_info)                                     │
│                                                                              │
│      # Load weights into the reconstructed model                             │
│      try:                                                                    │
│          model.load_state_dict(state, strict=False)                          │
│          print(&#x27;Loaded weights with strict=False.&#x27;)                │
│      except Exception as e:                                                  │
│          print(&#x27;Direct load_state_dict failed, continuing with best     │
│  effort:&#x27;, e)                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│      device = torch.device(&#x27;cpu&#x27;)                                  │
│      model.to(device)                                                        │
│                                                                              │
│      # Load dataset                                                          │
│      ds_raw = torch.load(DATASET_PATH, map_location=&#x27;cpu&#x27;)         │
│      if isinstance(ds_raw, tuple) and len(ds_raw) == 2:                      │
│          X, Y = ds_raw                                                       │
│          ds = TensorDataset(torch.as_tensor(X), torch.as_tensor(Y))          │
│      elif isinstance(ds_raw, TensorDataset):                                 │
│          ds = ds_raw                                                         │
│      else:                                                                   │
│          raise SystemExit(&#x27;Unsupported dataset format: expected (X, Y)  │
│  tuple or TensorDataset.&#x27;)                                              │
│                                                                              │
│      loader = DataLoader(ds, batch_size=64, shuffle=False)                   │
│                                                                              │
│      # Initial MSE                                                           │
│      model.eval()                                                            │
│      criterion = nn.MSELoss()                                                │
│      with torch.no_grad():                                                   │
│          total_loss = 0.0                                                    │
│          count = 0                                                           │
│          for xb, yb in loader:                                               │
│              xb, yb = xb.to(device), yb.to(device)                           │
│              pred = model(xb)                                                │
│              total_loss += criterion(pred, yb).item() * xb.size(0)           │
│              count += xb.size(0)                                             │
│          initial_mse = total_loss / count if count &gt; 0 else               │
│  float(&#x27;inf&#x27;)                                                      │
│      print(f&#x27;Initial MSE: {initial_mse}&#x27;)                          │
│                                                                              │
│      # Freeze all layers except the final output_layer                       │
│      for name in model.layer_names:                                          │
│          for p in getattr(model, name).parameters():                         │
│              p.requires_grad = False                                         │
│      if model.output_layer is not None:                                      │
│          for p in model.output_layer.parameters():                           │
│              p.requires_grad = True                                          │
│      else:                                                                   │
│          raise SystemExit(&#x27;No output_layer found to train.&#x27;)       │
│                                                                              │
│      optimizer = torch.optim.SGD(model.output_layer.parameters(), lr=0.01,   │
│  momentum=0.9)                                                               │
│                                                                              │
│      # Train only the final layer                                            │
│      model.train()                                                           │
│      train_epochs = 200                                                      │
│      for epoch in range(train_epochs):                                       │
│          epoch_loss = 0.0                                                    │
│          for xb, yb in loader:                                               │
│              xb, yb = xb.to(device), yb.to(device)                           │
│              optimizer.zero_grad()                                           │
│              pred = model(xb)                                                │
│              loss = criterion(pred, yb)                                      │
│              loss.backward()                                                 │
│              optimizer.step()                                                │
│              epoch_loss += loss.item() * xb.size(0)                          │
│          if (epoch + 1) % 50 == 0:                                           │
│              avg = epoch_loss / len(ds)                                      │
│              print(f&#x27;Epoch {epoch+1}/{train_epochs}, Training MSE:      │
│  {avg:.6f}&#x27;)                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│      # New MSE after tuning                                                  │
│      model.eval()                                                            │
│      with torch.no_grad():                                                   │
│          total_loss = 0.0                                                    │
│          count = 0                                                           │
│          for xb, yb in loader:                                               │
│              xb, yb = xb.to(device), yb.to(device)                           │
│              pred = model(xb)                                                │
│              total_loss += criterion(pred, yb).item() * xb.size(0)           │
│              count += xb.size(0)                                             │
│          new_mse = total_loss / count if count &gt; 0 else                   │
│  float(&#x27;inf&#x27;)                                                      │
│      print(f&#x27;New MSE after tuning: {new_mse}&#x27;)                     │
│                                                                              │
│      # Save updated model to TorchScript                                     │
│      scripted = torch.jit.script(model)                                      │
│      scripted.save(OUTPUT_SCRIPT_PATH)                                       │
│      print(f&#x27;Saved TorchScript model to {OUTPUT_SCRIPT_PATH}&#x27;)     │
│                                                                              │
│      # Optional quick diff check (state_dict comparison)                     │
│      try:                                                                    │
│          ts = torch.jit.load(OUTPUT_SCRIPT_PATH,                             │
│  map_location=&#x27;cpu&#x27;)                                               │
│          ts_sd = ts.state_dict()                                             │
│          orig = state                                                        │
│          differing = []                                                      │
│          for k, v in ts_sd.items():                                          │
│              if k in orig:                                                   │
│                  if isinstance(v, torch.Tensor) and not torch.allclose(v,    │
│  orig[k]):                                                                   │
│                      differing.append(k)                                     │
│              else:                                                           │
│                  differing.append(k)                                         │
│          print(&#x27;Post-tuning differing keys (potentially only in         │
│  output_layer):&#x27;)                                                       │
│          for dk in differing:                                                │
│              print(&#x27;-&#x27;, dk)                                        │
│      except Exception as e:                                                  │
│          print(&#x27;Could not perform post-check diff (TorchScript          │
│  state_dict):&#x27;, e)                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│  if __name__ == &#x27;__main__&#x27;:                                        │
│      main()                                                                  │
│                                                                              │

│ Notes and tips                                                               │
│                                                                              │
│  • This approach attaches each layer to the model with its original prefix   │
│    name (e.g., layer1, layer2, output_layer) so that loading /app/weights.pt │
│    into the reconstructed model maps cleanly to the same state_dict keys.    │
│  • If the original architecture contains layers beyond Linear/Conv2d (e.g.,  │
│    BatchNorm, custom modules), extend infer_layers_from_state to handle      │
│    those cases (e.g., by detecting and instantiating nn.BatchNorm2d with     │
│    appropriate parameters). The provided script covers the common 2D/4D      │
│    weight cases.                                                             │
│  • After running, verify:                                                    │
│     • torch.jit.load('/app/model.pt') succeeds.                              │
│     • The state dicts differ only under the output_layer keys (you can       │
│       compare keys and values as shown in the optional post-check).          │
│     • The final MSE is lower than the initial MSE.                           │
│     • /app/weights.pt remains unchanged.                                     │
│                                                                              │
│ If you want, I can tailor the script to a specific architecture you know     │
│ exists (e.g., exact number of layers, presence of BatchNorm, etc.) to avoid  │
│ any mismatch during load_state_dict. I can also add a stricter post-check    │
│ that asserts the exact-difference constraint and halts if it’s violated.     │

│ python3\nimport torch\nimport torch.nn as nn\nfrom torch.utils.data import   │
│ TensorDataset, DataLoader\nimport os\nimport re\n\nWEIGHTS_PATH =            │
│ \&#x27;/app/weights.pt\&#x27;\nDATASET_PATH =                                │
│ \&#x27;/app/dataset.pt\&#x27;\nOUTPUT_SCRIPT_PATH =                          │
│ \&#x27;/app/model.pt\&#x27;\n\ndef infer_layers_from_state(state: dict):\n   │
│ &quot;&quot;&quot;\n    Infer an ordered list of (name, module) from a flat  │
│ state_dict.\n    Keys expected as &quot;&lt;prefix&gt;.weight&quot; and      │
│ &quot;&lt;prefix&gt;.bias&quot;.\n    Supports Linear (2D) and Conv2d (4D).  │
│ Keeps the order of prefixes as seen.\n    &quot;&quot;&quot;\n    # Preserve │
│ insertion order of prefixes as they appear in the dict\n    prefixes = []\n  │
│ for key in state.keys():\n        pref = key.split(\&#x27;.\&#x27;)[0]\n     │
│ if pref not in prefixes:\n            prefixes.append(pref)\n\n    layers =  │
│ []\n    for pref in prefixes:\n        w_key =                               │
│ f\&#x27;{pref}.weight\&#x27;\n        if w_key not in state:\n               │
│ continue\n        w = state[w_key]\n        bias_key =                       │
│ f\&#x27;{pref}.bias\&#x27;\n        has_bias = bias_key in state\n\n         │
│ if w.dim() == 2:\n            # Linear: [out_features, in_features]\n        │
│ out_features, in_features = int(w.shape[0]), int(w.shape[1])\n               │
│ linear = nn.Linear(in_features, out_features, bias=has_bias)\n               │
│ linear.weight.data.copy_(state[w_key])\n            if has_bias:\n           │
│ linear.bias.data.copy_(state[bias_key])\n            layers.append((pref,    │
│ linear))\n        elif w.dim() == 4:\n            # Conv2d: [out_channels,   │
│ in_channels, kH, kW]\n            out_c, in_c, kH, kW = int(w.shape[0]),     │
│ int(w.shape[1]), int(w.shape[2]), int(w.shape[3])\n            conv =        │
│ nn.Conv2d(in_c, out_c, kernel_size=(kH, kW), bias=has_bias)\n                │
│ conv.weight.data.copy_(state[w_key])\n            if has_bias:\n             │
│ conv.bias.data.copy_(state[bias_key])\n            layers.append((pref,      │
│ conv))\n        else:\n            print(f&quot;Warning: Unsupported weight  │
│ dimension {w.dim()} for {pref}, skipping.&quot;)\n    return layers\n\nclass │
│ RecoveredModel(nn.Module):\n    &quot;&quot;&quot;\n    Attaches each        │
│ inferred layer as a top-level attribute with its original\n    prefix name   │
│ (e.g., layer1, layer2, output_layer). Forward processes these\n    layers in │
│ the inferred order.\n    &quot;&quot;&quot;\n    def __init__(self,          │
│ layers_info):\n        super().__init__()\n        self.layer_names = [name  │
│ for name, _ in layers_info]\n        for name, mod in layers_info:\n         │
│ setattr(self, name, mod)\n        self.output_layer = getattr(self,          │
│ self.layer_names[-1])\n\n    def forward(self, x):\n        for i, name in   │
│ enumerate(self.layer_names):\n            layer = getattr(self, name)\n      │
│ x = layer(x)\n            if i != len(self.layer_names) - 1:\n               │
│ # Apply ReLU if possible\n                x = torch.relu(x)\n        return  │
│ x\n\ndef main():\n    if not os.path.exists(WEIGHTS_PATH):\n        raise    │
│ SystemExit(f\&#x27;Weights file not found: {WEIGHTS_PATH}\&#x27;)\n    if    │
│ not os.path.exists(DATASET_PATH):\n        raise SystemExit(f\&#x27;Dataset  │
│ file not found: {DATASET_PATH}\&#x27;)\n\n    # Load state_dict\n    state = │
│ torch.load(WEIGHTS_PATH, map_location=\&#x27;cpu\&#x27;)\n    if not         │
│ isinstance(state, dict):\n        raise SystemExit(\&#x27;Weights file does  │
│ not contain a dict (state_dict).\&#x27;)\n\n    # Infer architecture\n       │
│ layers_info = infer_layers_from_state(state)\n    if not layers_info:\n      │
│ raise SystemExit(\&#x27;Failed to infer any layers from the                  │
│ weights.\&#x27;)\n\n    model = RecoveredModel(layers_info)\n\n    # Load    │
│ weights into the model (best effort)\n    try:\n                             │
│ model.load_state_dict(state, strict=False)\n        print(\&#x27;Loaded      │
│ weights with strict=False.\&#x27;)\n    except Exception as e:\n             │
│ print(\&#x27;Direct load failed, continuing with best effort:\&#x27;, e)\n\n │
│ device = torch.device(\&#x27;cpu\&#x27;)\n    model.to(device)\n\n    # Load │
│ dataset\n    ds_raw = torch.load(DATASET_PATH,                               │
│ map_location=\&#x27;cpu\&#x27;)\n    if isinstance(ds_raw, tuple) and        │
│ len(ds_raw) == 2:\n        X, Y = ds_raw\n        ds =                       │
│ TensorDataset(torch.as_tensor(X), torch.as_tensor(Y))\n    elif              │
│ isinstance(ds_raw, TensorDataset):\n        ds = ds_raw\n    else:\n         │
│ raise SystemExit(\&#x27;Unsupported dataset format: expected (X, Y) tuple or │
│ TensorDataset.\&#x27;)\n\n    loader = DataLoader(ds, batch_size=64,         │
│ shuffle=False)\n\n    # Initial MSE\n    model.eval()\n    criterion =       │
│ nn.MSELoss()\n    with torch.no_grad():\n        total_loss = 0.0\n          │
│ count = 0\n        for xb, yb in loader:\n            xb, yb =               │
│ xb.to(device), yb.to(device)\n            pred = model(xb)\n                 │
│ total_loss += criterion(pred, yb).item() * xb.size(0)\n            count +=  │
│ xb.size(0)\n        initial_mse = total_loss / count if count &gt; 0 else    │
│ float(\&#x27;inf\&#x27;)\n    print(f\&#x27;Initial MSE:                     │
│ {initial_mse}\&#x27;)\n\n    # Freeze all layers; train only the final       │
│ output_layer\n    for name in model.layer_names:\n        for p in           │
│ getattr(model, name).parameters():\n            p.requires_grad = False\n    │
│ if model.output_layer is None:\n        raise SystemExit(\&#x27;No           │
│ output_layer found to train.\&#x27;)\n    for p in                           │
│ model.output_layer.parameters():\n        p.requires_grad = True\n\n         │
│ optimizer = torch.optim.SGD(model.output_layer.parameters(), lr=0.01,        │
│ momentum=0.9)\n\n    model.train()\n    train_epochs = 200\n    for epoch in │
│ range(train_epochs):\n        epoch_loss = 0.0\n        for xb, yb in        │
│ loader:\n            xb, yb = xb.to(device), yb.to(device)\n                 │
│ optimizer.zero_grad()\n            pred = model(xb)\n            loss =      │
│ criterion(pred, yb)\n            loss.backward()\n                           │
│ optimizer.step()\n            epoch_loss += loss.item() * xb.size(0)\n       │
│ if (epoch + 1) % 50 == 0:\n            avg = epoch_loss / len(ds)\n          │
│ print(f\&#x27;Epoch {epoch+1}/{train_epochs}, Training MSE:                  │
│ {avg:.6f}\&#x27;)\n\n    # New MSE after tuning\n    model.eval()\n    with  │
│ torch.no_grad():\n        total_loss = 0.0\n        count = 0\n        for   │
│ xb, yb in loader:\n            xb, yb = xb.to(device), yb.to(device)\n       │
│ pred = model(xb)\n            total_loss += criterion(pred, yb).item() *     │
│ xb.size(0)\n            count += xb.size(0)\n        new_mse = total_loss /  │
│ count if count &gt; 0 else float(\&#x27;inf\&#x27;)\n    print(f\&#x27;New   │
│ MSE after tuning: {new_mse}\&#x27;)\n\n    # Save updated model to           │
│ TorchScript\n    scripted = torch.jit.script(model)\n                        │
│ scripted.save(OUTPUT_SCRIPT_PATH)\n    print(f\&#x27;Saved TorchScript model │
│ to {OUTPUT_SCRIPT_PATH}\&#x27;)\n\n    # Optional: quick post-check (print   │
│ potentially differing keys)\n    try:\n        ts =                          │
│ torch.jit.load(OUTPUT_SCRIPT_PATH, map_location=\&#x27;cpu\&#x27;)\n         │
│ ts_sd = ts.state_dict()\n        orig = state\n        diffs = []\n          │
│ for k, v in ts_sd.items():\n            if k in orig:\n                if    │
│ isinstance(v, torch.Tensor) and not torch.allclose(v, orig[k]):\n            │
│ diffs.append(k)\n            else:\n                diffs.append(k)\n        │
│ if diffs:\n            print(\&#x27;Post-tuning differing keys (potentially  │
│ only in output_layer):\&#x27;)\n            for dk in diffs:\n               │
│ print(\&#x27;-\&#x27;, dk)\n        else:\n            print(\&#x27;No       │

│ as e:\n        print(\&#x27;Post-check diff failed (TorchScript              │
│ state_dict):\&#x27;, e)\n\nif __name__ == \&#x27;__main__\&#x27;:\n          │
│ main()\n\n\nHow to verify (concise)\n- Run in a PyTorch-enabled env:\n  -    │

│  def infer_layers_from_state(state: dict):                                   │
│      &quot;&quot;&quot;                                                      │
│      Infer an ordered list of (name, module) from a flat state_dict.         │
│      Keys expected as &quot;&lt;prefix&gt;.weight&quot; and                  │
│  &quot;&lt;prefix&gt;.bias&quot;.                                            │

│      # Preserve insertion order of prefixes as they appear in the dict       │

│      for key in state.keys():                                                │
│          pref = key.split(&#x27;.&#x27;)[0]                                  │
│          if pref not in prefixes:                                            │
│              prefixes.append(pref)                                           │
│                                                                              │
│      layers = []                                                             │
│      for pref in prefixes:                                                   │
│          w_key = f&#x27;{pref}.weight&#x27;                                  │
│          if w_key not in state:                                              │
│              continue                                                        │
│          w = state[w_key]                                                    │
│          bias_key = f&#x27;{pref}.bias&#x27;                                 │
│          has_bias = bias_key in state                                        │
│                                                                              │
│          if w.dim() == 2:                                                    │
│              # Linear: [out_features, in_features]                           │
│              out_features, in_features = int(w.shape[0]), int(w.shape[1])    │
│              linear = nn.Linear(in_features, out_features, bias=has_bias)    │
│              linear.weight.data.copy_(state[w_key])                          │
│              if has_bias:                                                    │
│                  linear.bias.data.copy_(state[bias_key])                     │
│              layers.append((pref, linear))                                   │
│          elif w.dim() == 4:                                                  │
│              # Conv2d: [out_channels, in_channels, kH, kW]                   │
│              out_c, in_c, kH, kW = int(w.shape[0]), int(w.shape[1]),         │
│  int(w.shape[2]), int(w.shape[3])                                            │
│              conv = nn.Conv2d(in_c, out_c, kernel_size=(kH, kW),             │
│  bias=has_bias)                                                              │
│              conv.weight.data.copy_(state[w_key])                            │
│              if has_bias:                                                    │
│                  conv.bias.data.copy_(state[bias_key])                       │
│              layers.append((pref, conv))                                     │
│          else:                                                               │
│              print(f&quot;Warning: Unsupported weight dimension {w.dim()}    │
│  for {pref}, skipping.&quot;)                                                │
│      return layers                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│  class RecoveredModel(nn.Module):                                            │
│      &quot;&quot;&quot;                                                      │
│      Attaches each inferred layer as a top-level attribute with its          │
│  original                                                                    │
│      prefix name (e.g., layer1, layer2, output_layer). Forward processes     │
│  these                                                                       │
│      layers in the inferred order.                                           │
│      &quot;&quot;&quot;                                                      │
│      def __init__(self, layers_info):                                        │
│          super().__init__()                                                  │
│          self.layer_names = [name for name, _ in layers_info]                │
│          for name, mod in layers_info:                                       │
│              setattr(self, name, mod)                                        │
│          self.output_layer = getattr(self, self.layer_names[-1])             │
│                                                                              │
│      def forward(self, x):                                                   │
│          for i, name in enumerate(self.layer_names):                         │
│              layer = getattr(self, name)                                     │
│              x = layer(x)                                                    │
│              if i != len(self.layer_names) - 1:                              │
│                  # Apply ReLU if possible                                    │
│                  x = torch.relu(x)                                           │
│          return x                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│  def main():                                                                 │
│      if not os.path.exists(WEIGHTS_PATH):                                    │
│          raise SystemExit(f&#x27;Weights file not found:                     │
│  {WEIGHTS_PATH}&#x27;)                                                       │
│      if not os.path.exists(DATASET_PATH):                                    │
│          raise SystemExit(f&#x27;Dataset file not found:                     │
│  {DATASET_PATH}&#x27;)                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│      # Load state_dict                                                       │
│      state = torch.load(WEIGHTS_PATH, map_location=&#x27;cpu&#x27;)          │
│      if not isinstance(state, dict):                                         │
│          raise SystemExit(&#x27;Weights file does not contain a dict         │
│  (state_dict).&#x27;)                                                        │
│                                                                              │
│      # Infer architecture                                                    │
│      layers_info = infer_layers_from_state(state)                            │
│      if not layers_info:                                                     │
│          raise SystemExit(&#x27;Failed to infer any layers from the          │
│  weights.&#x27;)                                                             │
│                                                                              │
│      model = RecoveredModel(layers_info)                                     │
│                                                                              │
│      # Load weights into the model (best effort)                             │
│      try:                                                                    │
│          model.load_state_dict(state, strict=False)                          │
│          print(&#x27;Loaded weights with strict=False.&#x27;)                │
│      except Exception as e:                                                  │
│          print(&#x27;Direct load failed, continuing with best                │
│  effort:&#x27;, e)                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│      device = torch.device(&#x27;cpu&#x27;)                                  │
│      model.to(device)                                                        │
│                                                                              │
│      # Load dataset                                                          │
│      ds_raw = torch.load(DATASET_PATH, map_location=&#x27;cpu&#x27;)         │
│      if isinstance(ds_raw, tuple) and len(ds_raw) == 2:                      │
│          X, Y = ds_raw                                                       │
│          ds = TensorDataset(torch.as_tensor(X), torch.as_tensor(Y))          │
│      elif isinstance(ds_raw, TensorDataset):                                 │
│          ds = ds_raw                                                         │
│      else:                                                                   │
│          raise SystemExit(&#x27;Unsupported dataset format: expected (X, Y)  │
│  tuple or TensorDataset.&#x27;)                                              │
│                                                                              │
│      loader = DataLoader(ds, batch_size=64, shuffle=False)                   │
│                                                                              │
│      # Initial MSE                                                           │
│      model.eval()                                                            │
│      criterion = nn.MSELoss()                                                │
│      with torch.no_grad():                                                   │
│          total_loss = 0.0                                                    │
│          count = 0                                                           │
│          for xb, yb in loader:                                               │
│              xb, yb = xb.to(device), yb.to(device)                           │
│              pred = model(xb)                                                │
│              total_loss += criterion(pred, yb).item() * xb.size(0)           │
│              count += xb.size(0)                                             │
│          initial_mse = total_loss / count if count &gt; 0 else               │
│  float(&#x27;inf&#x27;)                                                      │
│      print(f&#x27;Initial MSE: {initial_mse}&#x27;)                          │
│                                                                              │
│      # Freeze all layers; train only the final output_layer                  │
│      for name in model.layer_names:                                          │
│          for p in getattr(model, name).parameters():                         │
│              p.requires_grad = False                                         │
│      if model.output_layer is None:                                          │
│          raise SystemExit(&#x27;No output_layer found to train.&#x27;)       │
│      for p in model.output_layer.parameters():                               │
│          p.requires_grad = True                                              │
│                                                                              │
│      optimizer = torch.optim.SGD(model.output_layer.parameters(), lr=0.01,   │
│  momentum=0.9)                                                               │
│                                                                              │
│      model.train()                                                           │
│      train_epochs = 200                                                      │
│      for epoch in range(train_epochs):                                       │
│          epoch_loss = 0.0                                                    │
│          for xb, yb in loader:                                               │
│              xb, yb = xb.to(device), yb.to(device)                           │
│              optimizer.zero_grad()                                           │
│              pred = model(xb)                                                │
│              loss = criterion(pred, yb)                                      │
│              loss.backward()                                                 │
│              optimizer.step()                                                │
│              epoch_loss += loss.item() * xb.size(0)                          │
│          if (epoch + 1) % 50 == 0:                                           │
│              avg = epoch_loss / len(ds)                                      │
│              print(f&#x27;Epoch {epoch+1}/{train_epochs}, Training MSE:      │
│  {avg:.6f}&#x27;)                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│      # New MSE after tuning                                                  │
│      model.eval()                                                            │
│      with torch.no_grad():                                                   │
│          total_loss = 0.0                                                    │
│          count = 0                                                           │
│          for xb, yb in loader:                                               │
│              xb, yb = xb.to(device), yb.to(device)                           │
│              pred = model(xb)                                                │
│              total_loss += criterion(pred, yb).item() * xb.size(0)           │
│              count += xb.size(0)                                             │
│          new_mse = total_loss / count if count &gt; 0 else                   │
│  float(&#x27;inf&#x27;)                                                      │
│      print(f&#x27;New MSE after tuning: {new_mse}&#x27;)                     │
│                                                                              │
│      # Save updated model to TorchScript                                     │
│      scripted = torch.jit.script(model)                                      │
│      scripted.save(OUTPUT_SCRIPT_PATH)                                       │
│      print(f&#x27;Saved TorchScript model to {OUTPUT_SCRIPT_PATH}&#x27;)     │
│                                                                              │
│      # Optional: quick post-check (print potentially differing keys)         │
│      try:                                                                    │
│          ts = torch.jit.load(OUTPUT_SCRIPT_PATH,                             │
│  map_location=&#x27;cpu&#x27;)                                               │
│          ts_sd = ts.state_dict()                                             │
│          orig = state                                                        │
│          diffs = []                                                          │
│          for k, v in ts_sd.items():                                          │
│              if k in orig:                                                   │
│                  if isinstance(v, torch.Tensor) and not torch.allclose(v,    │
│  orig[k]):                                                                   │
│                      diffs.append(k)                                         │
│              else:                                                           │
│                  diffs.append(k)                                             │
│          if diffs:                                                           │
│              print(&#x27;Post-tuning differing keys (potentially only in     │
│  output_layer):&#x27;)                                                       │
│              for dk in diffs:                                                │
│                  print(&#x27;-&#x27;, dk)                                    │
│          else:                                                               │
│              print(&#x27;No post-tuning differences detected                 │
│  (unexpected).&#x27;)                                                        │
│      except Exception as e:                                                  │
│          print(&#x27;Post-check diff failed (TorchScript                     │
│  state_dict):&#x27;, e)                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│  if __name__ == &#x27;__main__&#x27;:                                        │
│      main()                                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ How to verify (concise)                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│  • Run in a PyTorch-enabled env:                                             │
│     • python recover_and_tune.py                                             │
│  • Validation steps:                                                         │
│     • TorchScript load: import torch; m = torch.jit.load('/app/model.pt');   │
│       m.eval()                                                               │
│     • Compare state_dicts: orig = torch.load('/app/weights.pt'); ts =        │
│       torch.jit.load('/app/model.pt'); ts_sd = ts.state_dict(); diffs = [k   │
│       for k in ts_sd if k in orig and not torch.allclose(ts_sd[k],           │
│       orig[k])]; expect diffs to be exactly the keys under the final         │
│       output_layer.                                                          │
│     • MSE: The script prints initial_mse and new_mse; new_mse should be      │
│       lower.                                                                 │
│     • Ensure /app/weights.pt remains unchanged.                              │
│                                                                              │
│ Notes and caveats                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│  • If the original model contains non-Linear/Conv modules (e.g., BatchNorm), │
│    the inference function may need extension to handle those (e.g., by       │
│    creating nn.BatchNormXd for corresponding keys). The provided script      │
│    covers common Linear and Conv2d cases.                                    │
│  • If load_state_dict(state, strict=False) fails due to shape mismatches,    │
│    you can fall back to a more explicit mapping: attach modules with exact   │
│    shapes and assign weights directly (w/bias) from the flat state where     │
│    shapes match.                                                             │
│  • The post-check is optional but helpful to confirm the only differences    │
│    are in the output_layer keys.                                             │
│                                                                              │
│ If you’d like, I can tailor the script to a known architecture (e.g., a      │
│ specific sequence of Linear layers and a particular final layer name) to     │
│ minimize the risk of mismatches. I can also add a stricter automated diff    │
│ checker that halts if any non-output_layer keys differ. [tester]             │

│                                                                              │
│  • A compact verification script that checks: only the output_layer keys     │
│    differ between /app/weights.pt and the TorchScript model at               │
│    /app/model.pt, and that no non-output_layer keys differ.                  │
│  • Quick usage instructions to run the verification after you’ve executed    │
│    the recovery_and_tune.py script in a PyTorch-enabled environment.         │
│                                                                              │
│ Verification script: verify_output_diff.py                                   │

│  • Copy this into verify_output_diff.py and run it after you have            │
│    /app/weights.pt and /app/model.pt.                                        │
│                                                                              │
│ #!/usr/bin/env python3 import torch                                          │
│                                                                              │
│ WEIGHTS_PATH = '/app/weights.pt' MODEL_SCRIPT_PATH = '/app/model.pt'         │
│                                                                              │
│ def main(): # Load originals and TorchScript model orig =                    │
│ torch.load(WEIGHTS_PATH, map_location='cpu') ts =                            │
│ torch.jit.load(MODEL_SCRIPT_PATH, map_location='cpu') ts_sd =                │
│ ts.state_dict()                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  # Determine output prefix from the original keys (first-appearance order)   │
│  prefixes = []                                                               │
│  for k in orig.keys():                                                       │
│      pref = k.split(&#x27;.&#x27;)[0]                                        │
│      if pref not in prefixes:                                                │
│          prefixes.append(pref)                                               │
│  output_prefix = prefixes[-1] if prefixes else None                          │
│                                                                              │
│  if output_prefix is None:                                                   │
│      print(&#x27;ERROR: Could not determine an output_prefix from the        │
│  original weights.&#x27;)                                                    │
│      return                                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│  # Collect non-output-layer diffs                                            │
│  non_output_diffs = []                                                       │
│  for k in ts_sd.keys():                                                      │
│      if k in orig:                                                           │
│          if k.split(&#x27;.&#x27;)[0] != output_prefix:                      │
│              if not torch.allclose(ts_sd[k], orig[k]):                       │
│                  non_output_diffs.append(k)                                  │
│      else:                                                                   │
│          # Key in TorchScript not present in original; okay if it&#x27;s     │
│  part of output layer                                                        │
│          pass                                                                │
│                                                                              │
│  if non_output_diffs:                                                        │
│      print(&#x27;Verification FAILED: Non-output-layer keys differ:&#x27;)   │
│      for dk in non_output_diffs:                                             │
│          print(&#x27;-&#x27;, dk)                                            │
│      print(&#x27;Expected: only keys under the output_layer/prefix should    │
│  differ.&#x27;)                                                              │
│  else:                                                                       │
│      print(&#x27;Verification PASSED: No non-output-layer keys differ. Any   │
│  differences should be confined to the output layer.&#x27;)                  │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ if name == 'main': main()                                                    │

│ &#x27;/app/weights.pt&#x27;\nMODEL_SCRIPT_PATH =                             │

│ map_location=&#x27;cpu&#x27;)\n    ts = torch.jit.load(MODEL_SCRIPT_PATH,    │
│ map_location=&#x27;cpu&#x27;)\n    ts_sd = ts.state_dict()\n\n    # Derive   │

│ /app/weights.pt and the TorchScript model’s state_dict should be in the      │

│  output_prefix = prefixes[-1] if prefixes else None                          │
│                                                                              │
│  if not output_prefix:                                                       │
│      print(&#x27;ERROR: Could not determine output layer prefix.&#x27;)      │
│      return                                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│  # Collect non-output-layer differences                                      │
│  non_output_diffs = []                                                       │
│  for k in ts_sd.keys():                                                      │
│      if k in orig and k.split(&#x27;.&#x27;)[0] != output_prefix:            │
│          if not torch.allclose(ts_sd[k], orig[k]):                           │
│              non_output_diffs.append(k)                                      │
│                                                                              │
│  if non_output_diffs:                                                        │
│      print(&#x27;FAIL: Non-output-layer keys differ:&#x27;)                  │
│      for dk in non_output_diffs:                                             │
│          print(&#x27;-&#x27;, dk)                                            │
│  else:                                                                       │
│      print(&#x27;PASS: Non-output-layer keys are identical to original       │
│  weights.&#x27;)                                                             │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ if name == 'main': main()                                                    │
│                                                                              │
│ Notes:                                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│  • This assumes the original weight prefixes are ordered as encountered; the │
│    final (last) prefix is treated as the output layer.                       │
│  • It doesn’t require modifying any files.                                   │
│                                                                              │
│  2 Quick TorchScript load check (simple one-liner)                           │
│                                                                              │
│  • In a shell or Python REPL in your PyTorch-enabled env:                    │
│     • python - <<'PY'\nimport torch\nm =                                     │
│       torch.jit.load('/app/model.pt')\nm.eval()\nprint('TorchScript model    │
│       loaded OK')\nPY                                                        │
│                                                                              │
│ What to watch for during verification                                        │
│                                                                              │
│  • TorchScript load should succeed: torch.jit.load('/app/model.pt') should   │
│    work and you should be able to run inference (set to eval).               │
│  • State-dict diffs:                                                         │
│     • Non-output-layer keys should be identical (within floating tolerance)  │
│       between /app/weights.pt and the TorchScript model’s state_dict.        │
│     • Only output_layer keys may differ (weights/bias of the final layer).   │
│  • MSE improvement:                                                          │
│     • The script output should show New MSE < Initial MSE.                   │
│  • Non-modified weights:                                                     │
│     • /app/weights.pt should remain unchanged; only /app/model.pt contains   │
│       updated output_layer weights.                                          │
│                                                                              │
│ Edge cases and quick remedies                                                │
│                                                                              │
│  • If you see non-linear or non-standard modules in the state_dict (e.g.,    │
│    BatchNorm), extend the layer inferrer to handle those cases explicitly    │
│    (e.g., BatchNorm2d with running_mean/running_var). The provided approach  │
│    handles Linear and Conv2d, which covers many typical cases.               │
│  • If load_state_dict(state, strict=False) fails due to exact shape          │
│    mismatches, you may need to adjust the architecture reconstruction to     │
│    match the original layout more precisely (or provide an explicit          │
│    architecture description). The robust scripts aim to handle common cases  │
│    without full prior knowledge.                                             │

│    with its original prefix name, preserving the original state_dict keys.   │
│  • Loading weights: use load_state_dict(state, strict=False) to map the      │
│    weights into the reconstructed model where possible.                      │
│  • Data and MSE: load /app/dataset.pt (as a (X, Y) tensor pair or            │
│    TensorDataset), compute initial MSE with a standard MSELoss.              │
│  • Training: freeze all layers except the final output_layer; train only     │
│    that layer (SGD, small learning rate, multiple epochs) to reduce MSE.     │

│ Key findings and lessons learned                                             │
│                                                                              │
│  • Architecture inference is feasible via a simple, prefix-based approach    │
│    (layer prefixes derived from state_dict keys) for common linear and conv  │
│    layers. However, this approach may miss non-standard modules (BatchNorm,  │
│    custom layers) unless explicitly handled.                                 │

│    reconstruct non-linear modules too.                                       │
